limit.\n136\tfunc (s *State) AddCommandEntry(cmd string, userID int64, success bool) {\n137\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n138\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n139\t\tentry := CommandEntry{\n140\t\t\tTime:    time.Now().Format(\"15:04:05\"),\n141\t\t\tCommand: cmd,\n142\t\t\tUserID:  userID,\n143\t\t\tSuccess: success,\n144\t\t}\n145\t\ts.commandLog = append(s.commandLog, entry)\n146\t\tif len(s.commandLog) > maxCmdEntries {\n147\t\t\ts.commandLog = s.commandLog[1:]\n148\t\t}\n149\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\t// UpdateSession updates a session in the state.\n153\tfunc (s *State) UpdateSession(chatID, threadID int64, sessionID, topic, model, status, notifMode, cwd string, msgCount int, cost float64) {\n154\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n155\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n156\t\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d:%d\", chatID, threadID)\n157\t\tsession := s.sessions[key]\n158\t\tif session == nil {\n159\t\t\tsession = &SessionInfo{\n160\t\t\t\tChatID:     chatID,\n161\t\t\t\tThreadID:   threadID,\n162\t\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n163\t\t\t\tLastActive: time.Now(),\n164\t\t\t}\n165\t\t\ts.sessions[key] = session\n166\t\t}\n167\t\tsession.Topic = topic\n168\t\tsession.Model = model\n169\t\tsession.Status = status\n170\t\tsession.NotificationMode = notifMode\n171\t\tsession.CWD = cwd\n172\t\tsession.MessageCount = msgCount\n173\t\tsession.TotalCostUSD = cost\n174\t\tsession.LastActive = time.Now()\n175\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n176\t}\n177\t\n178\t// RemoveSession removes a session from the state.\n179\tfunc (s *State) RemoveSession(chatID, threadID int64) {\n180\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n181\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n182\t\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d:%d\", chatID, threadID)\n183\t\tdelete(s.sessions, key)\n184\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n185\t}\n186\t\n187\t// UpdateHealth updates the health status.\n188\tfunc (s *State) UpdateHealth(healthy bool, checks []HealthCheck) {\n189\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n190\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n191\t\ts.health.Healthy = healthy\n192\t\ts.health.Checks = checks\n193\t\ts.health.Timestamp = time.Now()\n194\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n195\t}\n196\t\n197\t// GetSessions returns a copy of the current sessions, sorted by LastActive (most recent first).\n198\tfunc (s *State) GetSessions() []*SessionInfo {\n199\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n200\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n201\t\tsessions := make([]*SessionInfo, 0, len(s.sessions))\n202\t\tfor _, sess := range s.sessions {\n203\t\t\tsessions = append(sessions, sess)\n204\t\t}\n205\t\t// Sort by LastActive (most recent first)\n206\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(sessions)-1; i++ {\n207\t\t\tfor j := i + 1; j < len(sessions); j++ {\n208\t\t\t\tif sessions[j].LastActive.After(sessions[i].LastActive) {\n209\t\t\t\t\tsessions[i], sessions[j] = sessions[j], sessions[i]\n210\t\t\t\t}\n211\t\t\t}\n212\t\t}\n213\t\treturn sessions\n214\t}\n215\t\n216\t// GetHealth returns a copy of the health status.\n217\tfunc (s *State) GetHealth() HealthStatus {\n218\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n219\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n220\t\treturn s.health\n221\t}\n222\t\n223\t// GetMessageLog returns a copy of the message log.\n224\tfunc (s *State) GetMessageLog() []LogEntry {\n225\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n226\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n227\t\tlog := make([]LogEntry, len(s.messageLog))\n228\t\tcopy(log, s.messageLog)\n229\t\treturn log\n230\t}\n231\t\n232\t// GetCommandLog returns a copy of the command log.\n233\tfunc (s *State) GetCommandLog() GetTotalCost returns the total cost across all sessions.\n242\tfunc (s *State) GetTotalCost() float64 {\n243\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n244\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n245\t\ttotal := 0.0\n246\t\tfor _, sess := range s.sessions {\n247\t\t\ttotal += sess.TotalCostUSD\n248\t\t}\n249\t\treturn total\n250\t}\n251\t\n252\t// GetActiveMessageCount returns the count of active messages.\n253\tfunc (s *State) GetActiveMessageCount() int {\n254\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n255\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n256\t\treturn s.activeMessageCount\n257\t}\n258\t\n259\t// IncrementActiveMessageCount increments the active message count.\n260\tfunc (s *State) IncrementActiveMessageCount() {\n261\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n262\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n263\t\ts.activeMessageCount++\n264\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n265\t}\n266\t\n267\t// DecrementActiveMessageCount decrements the active message count.\n268\tfunc (s *State) DecrementActiveMessageCount() {\n269\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n270\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n271\t\tif s.activeMessageCount > 0 {\n272\t\t\ts.activeMessageCount--\n273\t\t}\n274\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n275\t}\n276\t\n277\t// AddCostEntry adds a cost entry with timestamp.\n278\tfunc (s *State) AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID int64, costUSD float64, model string) {\n279\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n280\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n281\t\tentry := CostEntry{\n282\t\t\tTime:     time.Now(),\n283\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n284\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n285\t\t\tCostUSD:  costUSD,\n286\t\t\tModel:    model,\n287\t\t}\n288\t\ts.costLog = append(s.costLog, entry)\n289\t\tif len(s.costLog) > maxCostEntries {\n290\t\t\ts.costLog = s.costLog[1:]\n291\t\t}\n292\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n293\t}\n294\t\n295\t// GetCostToday returns the total cost for today (UTC).\n296\tfunc (s *State) GetCostToday() float64 {\n297\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n298\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n299\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n300\t\ttodayStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n301\t\ttotal := 0.0\n302\t\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n303\t\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(todayStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(todayStart) {\n304\t\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n305\t\t\t}\n306\t\t}\n307\t\treturn total\n308\t}\n309\t\n310\t// GetCostThisHour returns the total cost for the current hour (UTC).\n311\tfunc (s *State) GetCostThisHour() float64 {\n312\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n313\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n314\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n315\t\thourStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), now.Hour(), 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n316\t\ttotal := 0.0\n317\t\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n318\t\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(hourStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(hourStart) {\n319\t\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n320\t\t\t}\n321\t\t}\n322\t\treturn total\n323\t}\n324\t\n325\t// tickMsg is sent periodically to update the UI.\n326\ttype tickMsg time.Time\n327\t\n328\t// eventReceivedMsg is sent when an event is received from the socket.\n329\ttype eventReceivedMsg Event\n330\t\n331\t// connectedMsg is sent when successfully connected to the event socket.\n332\ttype connectedMsg struct {\n333\t\tConn net.Conn\n334\t}\n335\t\n336\t// disconnectedMsg is sent when disconnected from the event socket.\n337\ttype disconnectedMsg struct {\n338\t\terr error\n339\t}\n340\t\n341\t// eventWithConnMsg carries an event along with its connection.\n342\ttype eventWithConnMsg struct {\n343\t\tEvent Event\n344\t\tConn  net.Conn\n345\t}\n346\t\n347\t// model is the Bubble Tea model for the dashboard.\n348\ttype model struct {\n349\t\tstate          *State\n350\t\tconn           net.Conn\n351\t\tsocketPath     string\n352\t\treconnectDelay time.Duration\n353\t\twidth          int\n354\t\theight         int\n355\t\tconnected      bool\n356\t\tlastError      error\n357\t\tquitting       bool\n358\t}\n359\t\n360\t// initialModel creates the initial model.\n361\tfunc initialModel(socketPath string) model {\n362\t\treturn model{\n363\t\t\tstate:          NewState(),\n364\t\t\tsocketPath:     socketPath,\n365\t\t\treconnectDelay: time.Second,\n366\t\t\twidth:          80,\n367\t\t\theight:         24,\n368\t\t\tconnected:      false,\n369\t\t}\n370\t}\n371\t\n372\t// Init initializes the model.\n373\tfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n374\t\treturn tea.Batch(\n375\t\t\tm.connectCmd(),\n376\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n377\t\t)\n378\t}\n379\t\n380\t// Update handles messages and updates the model.\n381\tfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n382\t\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n383\t\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n384\t\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n385\t\t\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n386\t\t\t\tm.quitting = true\n387\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n388\t\t\tcase \"r\":\n389\t\t\t\t// Force reconnect\n390\t\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n391\t\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n392\t\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n393\t\t\t\t}\n394\t\t\t\tm.connected = false\n395\t\t\t\treturn m, m.connectCmd()\n396\t\t\t}\n397\t\tcase connectedMsg:\n398\t\t\tm.connected = true\n399\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n400\t\t\tm.reconnectDelay = time.Second\n401\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n402\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n403\t\t\t}\n404\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n405\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n406\t\tcase disconnectedMsg:\n407\t\t\tm.connected = false\n408\t\t\tm.lastError = msg.err\n409\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n410\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n411\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n412\t\t\t}\n413\t\t\treturn m, tea.Tick(m.reconnectDelay, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n414\t\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{}\n415\t\t\t})\n416\t\tcase eventWithConnMsg:\n417\t\t\t// Store the connection and handle the event\n418\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n419\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n420\t\t\t}\n421\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n422\t\t\tm.connected = true\n423\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n424\t\t\tm.handleEvent(msg.Event)\n425\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n426\t\tcase eventReceivedMsg:\n427\t\t\tm.handleEvent(Event(msg))\n428\t\t\treturn m, m.readEventsCmd()\n429\t\tcase tickMsg:\n430\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n431\t\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n432\t\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n433\t\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n434\t\t\treturn m, nil\n435\t\t}\n436\t\treturn m, nil\n437\t}\n438\t\n439\t// getFloat64 extracts a float64 value from an event field.\n440\tfunc getFloat64(event Event, key string) float64 {\n441\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n442\t\t\tswitch val := v.(type) {\n443\t\t\tcase float64:\n444\t\t\t\treturn val\n445\t\t\tcase int:\n446\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n447\t\t\tcase int64:\n448\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n449\t\t\t}\n450\t\t}\n451\t\treturn 0\n452\t}\n453\t\n454\t// getInt64 extracts an int64 value from an event field.\n455\tfunc getInt64(event Event, key string) int64 {\n456\t\treturn int64(getFloat64(event, key))\n457\t}\n458\t\n459\t// getString extracts a string value from an event field.\n460\tfunc getString(event Event, key string) string {\n461\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n462\t\t\tif s, ok := v.(string); ok {\n463\t\t\t\treturn s\n464\t\t\t}\n465\t\t}\n466\t\treturn \"\"\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\t// getBool extracts a bool value from an event field.\n470\tfunc getBool(event Event, key string) bool {\n471\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n472\t\t\tif b, ok := v.(bool); ok {\n473\t\t\t\treturn b\n474\t\t\t}\n475\t\t}\n476\t\treturn false\n477\t}\n478\t\n479\t// handleEvent processes a single event from the stream.\n480\tfunc (m *model) handleEvent(event Event) {\n481\t\teventType := getString(event, \"type\")\n482\t\n483\t\tswitch eventType {\n484\t\t// Phase 6 Event Types\n485\t\tcase \"message_in\":\n486\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n487\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n488\t\t\tpreview := getString(event, \"preview\")\n489\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN %s %s: %s\", topic, user, preview))\n490\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n491\t\n492\t\tcase \"message_out\":\n493\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n494\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n495\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n496\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n497\t\t\ttokens := int(getFloat64(event, \"tokens\"))\n498\t\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n499\t\n500\t\t\tif status == \"streaming\" {\n501\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s streaming %d tokens\", topic, tokens))\n502\t\t\t} else if status == \"complete\" {\n503\t\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, \"\")\n504\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s done %d tokens $%.4f\", topic, tokens, costUSD))\n505\t\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n506\t\t\t}\n507\t\n508\t\tcase \"command\":\n509\t\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n510\t\t\targs := getString(event, \"args\")\n511\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n512\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n513\t\t\tresult := getString(event, \"result\")\n514\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command+\" \"+args, 0, result == \"ok\")\n515\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD %s %s %s %s → %s\", topic, user, command, args, result))\n516\t\n517\t\tcase \"session_update\":\n518\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n519\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n520\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n521\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n522\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n523\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, \"\", topic, model, status, \"live\", \"\", 0, 0)\n524\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"SESSION %s %s: %s (%s)\", topic, status, model, status))\n525\t\n526\t\tcase \"health\":\n527\t\t\tproxyOK := getBool(event, \"proxy_ok\")\n528\t\t\tdbOK := getBool(event, \"db_ok\")\n529\t\t\tproxyLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"proxy_latency_ms\")\n530\t\t\tdbLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"db_latency_ms\")\n531\t\n532\t\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{\n533\t\t\t\t{Name: \"proxy\", Healthy: proxyOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", proxyLatencyMs)},\n534\t\t\t\t{Name: \"database\", Healthy: dbOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", dbLatencyMs)},\n535\t\t\t}\n536\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(proxyOK && dbOK, checks)\n537\t\n538\t\t// Legacy Event Types (for backward compatibility)\n539\t\tcase \"session_created\", \"session_updated\":\n540\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n541\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n542\t\t\tsessionID := getString(event, \"session_id\")\n543\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n544\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n545\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n546\t\t\tnotifMode := getString(event, \"notification_mode\")\n547\t\t\tif notifMode == \"\" {\n548\t\t\t\tnotifMode = \"live\"\n549\t\t\t}\n550\t\t\tcwd := getString(event, \"cwd\")\n551\t\t\tmsgCount := int(getFloat64(event, \"message_count\"))\n552\t\t\tcost := getFloat64(event, \"total_cost_usd\")\n553\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, sessionID, topic, model, status, notifMode, cwd, msgCount, cost)\n554\t\n555\t\tcase \"session_closed\":\n556\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n557\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n558\t\t\tm.state.RemoveSession(chatID, threadID)\n559\t\n560\t\tcase \"message_received\":\n561\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n562\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n563\t\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n564\t\t\tcontentType := getString(event, \"content_type\")\n565\t\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n566\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN ch=%d th=%d msg=%d type=%s uid=%d\", chatID, threadID, messageID, contentType, userID))\n567\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n568\t\n569\t\tcase \"message_sent\":\n570\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n571\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n572\t\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n573\t\t\tpurpose := getString(event, \"purpose\")\n574\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT ch=%d th=%d msg=%d purp=%s\", chatID, threadID, messageID, purpose))\n575\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n576\t\n577\t\tcase \"command_executed\":\n578\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n579\t\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n580\t\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n581\t\t\tsuccess := getBool(event, \"success\")\n582\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command, userID, success)\n583\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD ch=%d %s uid=%d ok=%t\", chatID, command, userID, success))\n584\t\n585\t\tcase \"cost_recorded\":\n586\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n587\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n588\t\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n589\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n590\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, model)\n591\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"COST ch=%d th=%d $%.4f %s\", chatID, threadID, costUSD, model))\n592\t\n593\t\tcase \"health_check\":\n594\t\t\thealthy := getBool(event, \"healthy\")\n595\t\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{}\n596\t\t\tif c, ok := event[\"checks\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n597\t\t\t\tfor _, checkItem := range c {\n598\t\t\t\t\tif checkMap, ok := checkItem.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n599\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck := HealthCheck{}\n600\t\t\t\t\t\tif name, ok := checkMap[\"name\"].(string); ok {\n601\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Name = name\n602\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n603\t\t\t\t\t\tif h, ok := checkMap[\"healthy\"].(bool); ok {\n604\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Healthy = h\n605\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n606\t\t\t\t\t\tif msg, ok := checkMap[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n607\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Message = msg\n608\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n609\t\t\t\t\t\tchecks = append(checks, check)\n610\t\t\t\t\t}\n611\t\t\t\t}\n612\t\t\t}\n613\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(healthy, checks)\n614\t\n615\t\tcase \"system_error\":\n616\t\t\tcomponent := getString(event, \"component\")\n617\t\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n618\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"ERROR [%s] %s\", component, message))\n619\t\n620\t\tcase \"system_info\":\n621\t\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n622\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"INFO %s\", message))\n623\t\t}\n624\t}\n625\t\n626\t// View renders the UI.\n627\tfunc (m model) View() string {\n628\t\tif m.quitting {\n629\t\t\treturn \"Goodbye!\\n\"\n630\t\t}\n631\t\n632\t\t// Define styles\n633\t\ttitleStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n634\t\t\tBold(true).\n635\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"86\")). // Light blue\n636\t\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n637\t\n638\t\tpanelStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n639\t\t\tBorder(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).\n640\t\t\tBorderForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"238\")). // Grey\n641\t\t\tPadding(0, 1)\n642\t\n643\t\theaderStyle := s.costLog[1:]\n\t}\n\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n}\n\n// GetCostToday returns the total cost for today (UTC).\nfunc (s *State) GetCostToday() float64 {\n\ts.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\ttodayStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n\ttotal := 0.0\n\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(todayStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(todayStart) {\n\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn total\n}\n\n// GetCostThisHour returns the total cost for the current hour (UTC).\nfunc (s *State) GetCostThisHour() float64 {\n\ts.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\thourStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), now.Hour(), 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n\ttotal := 0.0\n\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(hourStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(hourStart) {\n\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn total\n}\n\n// tickMsg is sent periodically to update the UI.\ntype tickMsg time.Time\n\n// eventReceivedMsg is sent when an event is received from the socket.\ntype eventReceivedMsg Event\n\n// connectedMsg is sent when successfully connected to the event socket.\ntype connectedMsg struct {\n\tConn net.Conn\n}\n\n// disconnectedMsg is sent when disconnected from the event socket.\ntype disconnectedMsg struct {\n\terr error\n}\n\n// eventWithConnMsg carries an event along with its connection.\ntype eventWithConnMsg struct {\n\tEvent Event\n\tConn  net.Conn\n}\n\n// model is the Bubble Tea model for the dashboard.\ntype model struct {\n\tstate          *State\n\tconn           net.Conn\n\tsocketPath     string\n\treconnectDelay time.Duration\n\twidth          int\n\theight         int\n\tconnected      bool\n\tlastError      error\n\tquitting       bool\n}\n\n// initialModel creates the initial model.\nfunc initialModel(socketPath string) model {\n\treturn model{\n\t\tstate:          NewState(),\n\t\tsocketPath:     socketPath,\n\t\treconnectDelay: time.Second,\n\t\twidth:          80,\n\t\theight:         24,\n\t\tconnected:      false,\n\t}\n}\n\n// Init initializes the model.\nfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Batch(\n\t\tm.connectCmd(),\n\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t)\n}\n\n// Update handles messages and updates the model.\nfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n\t\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n\t\t\tm.quitting = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase \"r\":\n\t\t\t// Force reconnect\n\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tm.connected = false\n\t\t\treturn m, m.connectCmd()\n\t\t}\n\tcase connectedMsg:\n\t\tm.connected = true\n\t\tm.lastError = nil\n\t\tm.reconnectDelay = time.Second\n\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n\tcase disconnectedMsg:\n\t\tm.connected = false\n\t\tm.lastError = msg.err\n\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m, tea.Tick(m.reconnectDelay, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{}\n\t\t})\n\tcase eventWithConnMsg:\n\t\t// Store the connection and handle the event\n\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n\t\tm.connected = true\n\t\tm.lastError = nil\n\t\tm.handleEvent(msg.Event)\n\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n\tcase eventReceivedMsg:\n\t\tm.handleEvent(Event(msg))\n\t\treturn m, m.readEventsCmd()\n\tcase tickMsg:\n\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\n// getFloat64 extracts a float64 value from an event field.\nfunc getFloat64(event Event, key string) float64 {\n\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n\t\tswitch val := v.(type) {\n\t\tcase float64:\n\t\t\treturn val\n\t\tcase int:\n\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n\t\tcase int64:\n\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn 0\n}\n\n// getInt64 extracts an int64 value from an event field.\nfunc getInt64(event Event, key string) int64 {\n\treturn int64(getFloat64(event, key))\n}\n\n// getString extracts a string value from an event field.\nfunc getString(event Event, key string) string {\n\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n\t\tif s, ok := v.(string); ok {\n\t\t\treturn s\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// getBool extracts a bool value from an event field.\nfunc getBool(event Event, key string) bool {\n\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n\t\tif b, ok := v.(bool); ok {\n\t\t\treturn b\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// handleEvent processes a single event from the stream.\nfunc (m *model) handleEvent(event Event) {\n\teventType := getString(event, \"type\")\n\n\tswitch eventType {\n\t// Phase 6 Event Types\n\tcase \"message_in\":\n\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n\t\tpreview := getString(event, \"preview\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN %s %s: %s\", topic, user, preview))\n\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n\n\tcase \"message_out\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n\t\ttokens := int(getFloat64(event, \"tokens\"))\n\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n\n\t\tif status == \"streaming\" {\n\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s streaming %d tokens\", topic, tokens))\n\t\t} else if status == \"complete\" {\n\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, \"\")\n\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s done %d tokens $%.4f\", topic, tokens, costUSD))\n\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"command\":\n\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n\t\targs := getString(event, \"args\")\n\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n\t\tresult := getString(event, \"result\")\n\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command+\" \"+args, 0, result == \"ok\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD %s %s %s %s → %s\", topic, user, command, args, result))\n\n\tcase \"session_update\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, \"\", topic, model, status, \"live\", \"\", 0, 0)\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"SESSION %s %s: %s (%s)\", topic, status, model, status))\n\n\tcase \"health\":\n\t\tproxyOK := getBool(event, \"proxy_ok\")\n\t\tdbOK := getBool(event, \"db_ok\")\n\t\tproxyLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"proxy_latency_ms\")\n\t\tdbLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"db_latency_ms\")\n\n\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{\n\t\t\t{Name: \"proxy\", Healthy: proxyOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", proxyLatencyMs)},\n\t\t\t{Name: \"database\", Healthy: dbOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", dbLatencyMs)},\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(proxyOK && dbOK, checks)\n\n\t// Legacy Event Types (for backward compatibility)\n\tcase \"session_created\", \"session_updated\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\tsessionID := getString(event, \"session_id\")\n\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n\t\tnotifMode := getString(event, \"notification_mode\")\n\t\tif notifMode == \"\" {\n\t\t\tnotifMode = \"live\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tcwd := getString(event, \"cwd\")\n\t\tmsgCount := int(getFloat64(event, \"message_count\"))\n\t\tcost := getFloat64(event, \"total_cost_usd\")\n\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, sessionID, topic, model, status, notifMode, cwd, msgCount, cost)\n\n\tcase \"session_closed\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\tm.state.RemoveSession(chatID, threadID)\n\n\tcase \"message_received\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n\t\tcontentType := getString(event, \"content_type\")\n\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN ch=%d th=%d msg=%d type=%s uid=%d\", chatID, threadID, messageID, contentType, userID))\n\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n\n\tcase \"message_sent\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n\t\tpurpose := getString(event, \"purpose\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT ch=%d th=%d msg=%d purp=%s\", chatID, threadID, messageID, purpose))\n\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n\n\tcase \"command_executed\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n\t\tsuccess := getBool(event, \"success\")\n\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command, userID, success)\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD ch=%d %s uid=%d ok=%t\", chatID, command, userID, success))\n\n\tcase \"cost_recorded\":\n\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, model)\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"COST ch=%d th=%d $%.4f %s\", chatID, threadID, costUSD, model))\n\n\tcase \"health_check\":\n\t\thealthy := getBool(event, \"healthy\")\n\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{}\n\t\tif c, ok := event[\"checks\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n\t\t\tfor _, checkItem := range c {\n\t\t\t\tif checkMap, ok := checkItem.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tcheck := HealthCheck{}\n\t\t\t\t\tif name, ok := checkMap[\"name\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Name = name\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif h, ok := checkMap[\"healthy\"].(bool); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Healthy = h\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif msg, ok := checkMap[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Message = msg\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tchecks = append(checks, check)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(healthy, checks)\n\n\tcase \"system_error\":\n\t\tcomponent := getString(event, \"component\")\n\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"ERROR [%s] %s\", component, message))\n\n\tcase \"system_info\":\n\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"INFO %s\", message))\n\t}\n}\n\n// View renders the UI.\nfunc (m model) View() string {\n\tif m.quitting {\n\t\treturn \"Goodbye!\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Define styles\n\ttitleStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\tBold(true).\n\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"86\")). // Light blue\n\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n\n\tpanelStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\tBorder(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).\n\t\tBorderForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"238\")). // Grey\n\t\tPadding(0, 1)\n\n\theaderStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\tBold(true).\n\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"228\")). // Yellow\n\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n\n\tsuccessStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"142\")) // Green\n\terrorStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"203\"))   // Red\n\twarningStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"229\")) // Yellow\n\tinfoStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"39\"))    // Blue\n\tdimStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"241\"))    // Grey\n\tstatusStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)                       // Bold for status\n\n\t// Calculate layout\n\tleftWidth := m.width / 2\n\trightWidth := m.width - leftWidth - 4 // 4 for borders/gaps\n\n\tsessionHeight := m.height / 2\n\thealthHeight := 6\n\tlogHeight := m.height - sessionHeight - healthHeight - 4 // 4 for headers/gaps\n\n\t// Header\n\theader := titleStyle.Render(\"Telegram Claude Bridge Dashboard\")\n\tif m.connected {\n\t\theader += successStyle.Render(\" ● Connected\")\n\t} else {\n\t\theader += errorStyle.Render(\" ● Disconnected\")\n\t\tif m.lastError != nil {\n\t\t\theader += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" (%s)\", m.lastError))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\theader += dimStyle.Render(\" | Press 'q' to quit, 'r' to reconnect\")\n\n\t// Active Sessions Panel\n\tsessions := m.state.GetSessions()\n\tsessionsPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Active Sessions\") + \"\\n\"\n\tif len(sessions) == 0 {\n\t\tsessionsPanel += dimStyle.Render(\"No active sessions\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tfor i, sess := range sessions {\n\t\t\tif i >= sessionHeight-2 {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Color-coded status: green=idle, blue=processing, yellow=streaming, red=error\n\t\t\tstatusColor := successStyle\n\t\t\tif sess.Status == \"processing\" {\n\t\t\t\tstatusColor = infoStyle\n\t\t\t} else if sess.Status == \"streaming\" {\n\t\t\t\tstatusColor = warningStyle\n\t\t\t} else if sess.Status == \"error\" {\n\t\t\t\tstatusColor = errorStyle\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Calculate idle duration\n\t\t\tidleDuration := time.Since(sess.LastActive)\n\t\t\tidleStr := formatDuration(idleDuration)\n\n\t\t\t// Display topic name, or chat_id/thread_id if no topic\n\t\t\ttopicDisplay := sess.Topic\n\t\t\tif topicDisplay == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\ttopicDisplay = fmt.Sprintf(\"ch=%d/th=%d\", sess.ChatID, sess.ThreadID)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %-20s %s idle=%s\",\n\t\t\t\tstatusStyle.Render(sess.Status),\n\t\t\t\ttruncateString(topicDisplay, 20),\n\t\t\t\ttruncateString(sess.Model, 10),\n\t\t\t\tidleStr,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tsessionsPanel += statusColor.Render(line) + \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(sessions) > sessionHeight-2 {\n\t\t\tsessionsPanel += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"... and %d more\", len(sessions)-(sessionHeight-2)))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// System Health Panel\n\thealth := m.state.GetHealth()\n\thealthPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"System Health\") + \"\\n\"\n\tfor _, check := range health.Checks {\n\t\tcheckStyle := successStyle\n\t\tif !check.Healthy {\n\t\t\tcheckStyle = errorStyle\n\t\t}\n\t\tmsg := check.Message\n\t\tif msg == \"\" {\n\t\t\tmsg = \"ok\"\n\t\t}\n\t\thealthPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %-12s %s\\n\",\n\t\t\tcheckStyle.Render(statusIcon(check.Healthy)),\n\t\t\tcheck.Name+\":\", checkStyle.Render(msg),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\t// Messages In Flight Panel\n\tlogEntries := m.state.GetMessageLog()\n\tlogPanel := headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"Messages In Flight (%d active)\", m.state.GetActiveMessageCount())) + \"\\n\"\n\tstartIdx := len(logEntries) - logHeight + 1\n\tif startIdx < 0 {\n\t\tstartIdx = 0\n\t}\n\tfor i := startIdx; i < len(logEntries); i++ {\n\t\tlogPanel += dimStyle.Render(logEntries[i].Time+\" \") + logEntries[i].Msg + \"\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Command Log Panel\n\tcmdEntries := m.state.GetCommandLog()\n\tcmdPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Command Log\") + \"\\n\"\n\tcmdStartIdx := len(cmdEntries) - logHeight + 1\n\tif cmdStartIdx < 0 {\n\t\tcmdStartIdx = 0\n\t}\n\tfor i := cmdStartIdx; i < len(cmdEntries); i++ {\n\t\tcmd := cmdEntries[i]\n\t\tcmdStyle := successStyle\n\t\tif !cmd.Success {\n\t\t\tcmdStyle = errorStyle\n\t\t}\n\t\tcmdPanel += dimStyle.Render(cmd.Time+\" \") +\n\t\t\tcmdStyle.Render(truncateString(cmd.Command, 30)) +\n\t\t\tdimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" uid=%d\", cmd.UserID)) + \"\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Cost Tracker Panel\n\ttotalCost := m.state.GetTotalCost()\n\tcostToday := m.state.GetCostToday()\n\tcostThisHour := m.state.GetCostThisHour()\n\tactiveCount := len(sessions)\n\n\tcostPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Cost Tracker\") + \"\\n\"\n\tcostPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"Today: $%.4f  │  This Hour: $%.4f  │  Active: %d\\n\", costToday, costThisHour, activeCount)\n\tcostPanel += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"Total: $%.4f\\n\", totalCost))\n\tif len(sessions) > 0 {\n\t\tcostPanel += dimStyle.Render(\"Top sessions by cost:\\n\")\n\t\t// Sort sessions by cost\n\t\tsortedSessions := make([]*SessionInfo, len(sessions))\n\t\tcopy(sortedSessions, sessions)\n\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(sortedSessions)-1; i++ {\n\t\t\tfor j := i + 1; j < len(sortedSessions); j++ {\n\t\t\t\tif sortedSessions[j].TotalCostUSD > sortedSessions[i].TotalCostUSD {\n\t\t\t\t\tsortedSessions[i], sortedSessions[j] = sortedSessions[j], sortedSessions[i]\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(sortedSessions) && i < 3; i++ {\n\t\t\tsess := sortedSessions[i]\n\t\t\tcostPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"  ch=%d th=%d: $%.4f\\n\", sess.ChatID, sess.ThreadID, sess.TotalCostUSD)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Layout\n\tleftColumn := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left,\n\t\tpanelStyle.Width(leftWidth).Height(sessionHeight).Render(sessionsPanel),\n\t\tpanelStyle.Width(leftWidth).Height(healthHeight).Render(healthPanel),\n\t)\n\trightColumn := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left,\n\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(logHeight).Render(logPanel),\n\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(logHeight).Render(cmdPanel),\n\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(6).Render(costPanel),\n\t)\n\n\tmainContent := lipgloss.JoinHorizontal(lipgloss.Top, leftColumn, rightColumn)\n\n\treturn header + \"\\n\" + mainContent\n}\n\n// statusIcon returns a checkmark or cross icon.\nfunc statusIcon(healthy bool) string {\n\tif healthy {\n\t\treturn \"✓\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"✗\"\n}\n\n// truncateString truncates a string to a maximum length.\nfunc truncateString(s string, maxLen int) string {\n\tif len(s) <= maxLen {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\treturn s[:maxLen-1] + \"…\"\n}\n\n// formatDuration formats a duration as a human-readable string (e.g., \"5m\", \"2h30m\").\nfunc formatDuration(d time.Duration) string {\n\tif d < time.Minute {\n\t\treturn \"just now\"\n\t}\n\tif d < time.Hour {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dm\", int(d.Minutes()))\n\t}\n\tif d < 24*time.Hour {\n\t\thours := int(d.Hours())\n\t\tmins := int(d.Minutes()) % 60\n\t\tif mins > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dh%dm\", hours, mins)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dh\", hours)\n\t}\n\tdays := int(d.Hours() / 24)\n\thours := int(d.Hours()) % 24\n\tif hours > 0 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dd%dh\", days, hours)\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dd\", days)\n}\n\n// connectCmd attempts to connect to the event socket.\nfunc (m model) connectCmd() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tconn, err := net.Dial(\"unix\", m.socketPath)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"dial failed: %w\", err)}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn connectedMsg{Conn: conn}\n\t}\n}\n\n// waitForNextEvent waits for the next event on an existing connection.\nfunc waitForNextEvent(conn net.Conn) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tdecoder := json.NewDecoder(conn)\n\t\tvar event Event\n\t\tif err := decoder.Decode(&event); err != nil {\n\t\t\tconn.Close()\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"connection closed\")}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"decode failed: %w\", err)}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn eventReceivedMsg(event)\n\t}\n}\n\n// readEventsCmd reads events from the socket.\nfunc (m model) readEventsCmd() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tconn, err := net.Dial(\"unix\", m.socketPath)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"dial failed: %w\", err)}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdecoder := json.NewDecoder(conn)\n\t\tvar event Event\n\t\tif err := decoder.Decode(&event); err != nil {\n\t\t\tconn.Close()\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"connection closed\")}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"decode failed: %w\", err)}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Store connection for subsequent reads\n\t\treturn eventWithConnMsg{Event: event, Conn: conn}\n\t}\n}\n\n// tickCmd returns a command that sends tick messages periodically.\nfunc tickCmd() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(250*time.Millisecond, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n\t\treturn tickMsg(t)\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n\t// Parse command-line flags\n\tsocketPath :=
